1. Replace the entire <nav> block

html
Copy
Download
Run
<nav class="nav">
  <div class="wrap nav-in">
    <a href="#" class="brand"><span class="logo">JF</span> JSONForge</a>

    <div class="nav-right">
      <div class="nav-links">
        <a href="#tool">Tool</a>
        <a href="#why">Why us</a>
        <a href="#compare">Compare</a>
        <a href="#faq">FAQ</a>
      </div>

      <span class="nav-divider" aria-hidden="true"></span>

      <button class="theme-btn" id="themeBtn" aria-label="Toggle theme" title="Toggle dark / light mode">
        <!-- sun = shown in dark mode (click ‚Üí light) -->
        <svg class="icon-sun" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
          <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="4"/>
          <path d="M12 2v2M12 20v2M2 12h2M20 12h2M4.9 4.9l1.4 1.4M17.7 17.7l1.4 1.4M4.9 19.1l1.4-1.4M17.7 6.3l1.4-1.4"/>
        </svg>
        <!-- moon = shown in light mode (click ‚Üí dark) -->
        <svg class="icon-moon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round">
          <path d="M21 12.8A9 9 0 1111.2 3a7 7 0 009.8 9.8z"/>
        </svg>
      </button>
    </div>
  </div>
</nav>

2. Replace the /* ---------- NAV / BREADCRUMB ---------- */ CSS block

css
Copy
Download
/* ---------- NAV / BREADCRUMB ---------- */
.nav{border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);position:sticky;top:0;z-index:50;background:color-mix(in srgb,var(--bg) 78%,transparent);backdrop-filter:blur(20px) saturate(180%)}
.nav-in{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;height:62px;gap:16px}
.brand{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:-.02em;font-size:15px}
.logo{width:26px;height:26px;border-radius:8px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,var(--accent),var(--accent-2));display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:13px;color:#fff;font-weight:800}

/* right-hand cluster: links + divider + theme toggle (opposite the logo) */
.nav-right{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-left:auto}
.nav-links{display:flex;gap:4px;align-items:center}
.nav-links a{font-size:13px;color:var(--muted);padding:7px 12px;border-radius:8px;transition:.16s}
.nav-links a:hover{color:var(--text);background:var(--panel-2)}
.nav-divider{width:1px;height:20px;background:var(--border);margin:0 4px}

.theme-btn{
  width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:9px;border:1px solid var(--border);
  display:grid;place-items:center;color:var(--muted);transition:.16s;
  background:var(--panel-2);flex:0 0 auto;
}
.theme-btn:hover{color:var(--text);border-color:var(--border-2);background:var(--panel-3);transform:rotate(-8deg)}
.theme-btn svg{width:15px;height:15px;display:block}
html[data-theme="dark"]  .theme-btn .icon-moon{display:none}
html[data-theme="light"] .theme-btn .icon-sun{display:none}

/* keep the toggle reachable on small screens */
@media(max-width:680px){
  .nav-links{display:none}
  .nav-divider{display:none}
}

What changed

BeforeAfter
Theme button buried inside the .nav-links rowStandalone .nav-right cluster pinned to the far right with margin-left:auto
Single static sun iconSun in dark mode, moon in light mode ‚Äî swapped purely via CSS html[data-theme] selectors, so your existing JS toggle keeps working untouched
No visual separationThin .nav-divider rule between nav links and the toggle
Button looked flatSubtle panel background + a small rotate(-8deg) on hover for a bit of tactile delight
Cramped on phonesNav links collapse at ‚â§680px so the theme toggle stays tappable

No JavaScript changes are needed ‚Äî the existing #themeBtn click handler and localStorage persistence already target the button by ID, and the icon swap is handled entirely by CSS.

    <div class="sec-head">
      <span class="eyebrow">Why JSONForge</span>
      <h2>Built for engineers who are done with ad-cluttered tools</h2>
      <p>Legacy formatters were built in 2009. This one was built for 10MB payloads, modern browsers, and zero trust in third-party servers.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="grid4">
      <div class="card"><div class="ico">‚ö°</div><h3>Sub-millisecond parsing</h3><p>Background Web Worker parsing keeps the main thread at 60fps even on multi-megabyte payloads. No spinner, no freeze, no "script is unresponsive".</p></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="ico">üîí</div><h3>Zero server transmission</h3><p>Your JSON never leaves the tab. No fetch calls, no analytics on your payload, no logs. Works fully offline once loaded. GDPR &amp; SOC2 friendly by design.</p></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="ico">üå≥</div><h3>Deep tree inspection</h3><p>Collapse, expand and navigate nested objects with a real visual hierarchy. Instantly see array lengths, key counts and value types at a glance.</p></div>
      <div class="card"><div class="ico">üîÅ</div><h3>Multi-format conversion</h3><p>JSON ‚Üí YAML, CSV and XML in one click, with copy or download. Stop opening five different tabs to move data between formats.</p></div>
    </div>

    <div class="sec-head">
      <span class="eyebrow">How it works</span>
      <h2>Format, validate &amp; convert in four steps</h2>
      <p>No signup, no setup, no keyboard gymnastics required.</p>
    </div>
    <div class="steps">
      <div class="step"><div class="num">1</div><h4>Paste or drop your JSON</h4><p>Drop a <code>.json</code> file onto the input pane, or paste raw text. Counters show characters, bytes and lines live.</p></div>
      <div class="step"><div class="num">2</div><h4>Validate instantly</h4><p>Errors highlight the exact line and column with an actionable fix suggestion ‚Äî trailing commas, single quotes, bare keys, and more.</p></div>
      <div class="step"><div class="num">3</div><h4>Auto-Fix &amp; Prettify</h4><p>One click repairs broken JSON and re-indents it with 2 or 4 spaces. Or hit Minify to compress it for production payloads.</p></div>
      <div class="step"><div class="num">4</div><h4>Inspect &amp; export</h4><p>Switch to Tree View to explore structure, then copy or download as JSON, YAML, CSV or XML ‚Äî or generate a share link.</p></div>
    </div>

  <!-- FAQ -->
  <section id="faq" style="padding-top:0">
    <div class="sec-head">
      <span class="eyebrow">FAQ</span>
      <h2>Questions engineers actually ask</h2>
    </div>
    <div class="faq" id="faqList">
      <div class="fitem">
        <button class="fq">Is my JSON data uploaded to a server?<span class="plus"></span></button>
        <div class="fa"><p>No. Every byte of parsing, formatting, validation and conversion happens inside your browser using JavaScript and an inline Web Worker. There are no network calls containing your payload, no logging, and no storage. You can verify this by opening DevTools ‚Üí Network and watching it stay silent while you format.</p></div>
      </div>
      <div class="fitem">
        <button class="fq">What is the maximum JSON payload size?<span class="plus"></span></button>
        <div class="fa"><p>The formatter comfortably handles payloads up to <strong>10MB</strong>. Parsing and validation run off the main thread via a Web Worker, so the interface stays responsive. Syntax highlighting automatically throttles above 200KB to prevent DOM overhead from causing lag ‚Äî the data itself is still fully parsed and convertible.</p></div>
      </div>
      <div class="fitem">
        <button class="fq">How does the Auto-Fix engine work?<span class="plus"></span></button>
        <div class="fa"><p>Auto-Fix runs a character-level scanner across six repair passes: it strips <code>//</code> and <code>/* */</code> comments, converts single-quoted strings to double-quoted, escapes raw newlines inside strings, wraps bare object keys in quotes, removes trailing commas before <code>}</code> or <code>]</code>, and normalizes <code>True</code>/<code>False</code>/<code>None</code> to JSON literals. If the result still isn't valid, the tool tells you exactly where the remaining problem is.</p></div>
      </div>
      <div class="fitem">
        <button class="fq">Can I convert JSON to YAML, CSV or XML?<span class="plus"></span></button>
        <div class="fa"><p>Yes. After parsing, use the Download menu to export as <code>.json</code>, <code>.yaml</code>, <code>.csv</code> or <code>.xml</code>. CSV conversion automatically detects arrays of objects (using the union of all keys as headers) as well as arrays of arrays, and safely quotes values containing commas or quotes.</p></div>
      </div>
      <div class="fitem">
        <button class="fq">Does the tool work offline?<span class="plus"></span></button>
        <div class="fa"><p>Yes. Because there is no server round-trip, the tool keeps working after your connection drops or if you save the page locally. It's also safe for air-gapped and compliance-restricted environments.</p></div>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>
</div>

<script>
(function(){
"use strict";
const $ = s => document.querySelector(s);
const $$ = s => Array.from(document.querySelectorAll(s));

/* ================= THEME ================= */
const root = document.documentElement;
const savedTheme = (()=>{ try{ return localStorage.getItem('jf-theme'); }catch(e){ return null; } })();
if(savedTheme) root.setAttribute('data-theme', savedTheme);
$('#themeBtn').addEventListener('click', ()=>{
  const next = root.getAttribute('data-theme') === 'dark' ? 'light' : 'dark';
  root.setAttribute('data-theme', next);
  try{ localStorage.setItem('jf-theme', next); }catch(e){}
});

/* ================= TOAST ================= */
let toastTimer;
function toast(msg){
  const t = $('#toast');
  t.textContent = msg;
  t.classList.add('show');
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer = setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('show'), 2100);
}

/* ================= STATE ================= */
const els = {
  ta: $('#inTa'), pre: $('#inPre'), gutter: $('#inGutter'), gutterWrap: $('#inGutter').parentElement,
  out: $('#outPre'), outGutter: $('#outGutter'), outGutterWrap: $('#outGutterWrap'),
  tree: $('#treeView'), badge: $('#statusBadge'), stats: $('#stats'),
  errBar: $('#errBar'), errMsg: $('#errMsg'), outLabel: $('#outLabel')
};
const state = {
  mode: 'code',
  indent: 2,
  parsed: undefined,
  valid: false,
  rawOutput: '',
  errorLine: -1,
  lastError: null
};

/* ================= HIGHLIGHTER ================= */
const ESC = {'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;'};
const esc = s => s.replace(/[&<>]/g, c => ESC[c]);
const HL_CAP = 200000;

function highlightJson(src){
  if(src.length > HL_CAP) return esc(src);
  let out = '', i = 0, n = src.length;
  while(i < n){
    const c = src[i];
    if(c === '"'){
      let j = i + 1;
      while(j < n){
        if(src[j] === '\\'){ j += 2; continue; }
        if(src[j] === '"'){ j++; break; }
        if(src[j] === '\n'){ break; }
        j++;
      }
      const str = src.slice(i, j);
      let k = j;
      while(k < n && (src[k] === ' ' || src[k] === '\t')) k++;
      out += src[k] === ':'
        ? '<span class="tok-key">' + esc(str) + '</span>'
        : '<span class="tok-str">' + esc(str) + '</span>';
      i = j; continue;
    }
    if((c >= '0' && c <= '9') || (c === '-' && /[0-9]/.test(src[i+1] || ''))){
      const m = /^-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?(?:[eE][+-]?\d+)?/.exec(src.slice(i, i + 40));
      if(m){ out += '<span class="tok-num">' + m[0] + '</span>'; i += m[0].length; continue; }
    }
    if(/[A-Za-z_$]/.test(c)){
      const m = /^[A-Za-z_$][A-Za-z0-9_$]*/.exec(src.slice(i, i + 80));
      const w = m[0];
      if(w === 'true' || w === 'false') out += '<span class="tok-bool">' + w + '</span>';
      else if(w === 'null') out += '<span class="tok-null">' + w + '</span>';
      else out += '<span class="tok-err">' + esc(w) + '</span>';
      i += w.length; continue;
    }
    if(c === '{' || c === '}' || c === '[' || c === ']' || c === ',' || c === ':'){
      out += '<span class="tok-punc">' + esc(c) + '</span>'; i++; continue;
    }
    out += esc(c); i++;
  }
  return out;
}

function gutterHTML(text, errLine){
  const lines = text.split('\n').length;
  const cap = Math.min(lines, 8000);
  let h = '';
  for(let i = 1; i <= cap; i++){
    h += (i === errLine ? '<div class="errline">' + i + '</div>' : '<div>' + i + '</div>');
  }
  return h;
}

/* ================= INPUT RENDER ================= */
function renderInput(){
  const text = els.ta.value;
  els.pre.innerHTML = highlightJson(text) + '\n';
  els.gutter.innerHTML = gutterHTML(text, state.errorLine);
  syncScroll();
}
function syncScroll(){
  els.pre.scrollTop = els.ta.scrollTop;
  els.pre.scrollLeft = els.ta.scrollLeft;
  els.gutterWrap.scrollTop = els.ta.scrollTop;
}
els.ta.addEventListener('scroll', syncScroll, {passive:true});
els.ta.addEventListener('input', () => { schedule(); renderInput(); });

/* ================= PARSING (Web Worker) ================= */
let worker = null;
const workerSrc = `
self.onmessage = function(e){
  var id = e.data.id, text = e.data.text;
  try{
    JSON.parse(text);
    self.postMessage({ id: id, ok: true });
  }catch(err){
    self.postMessage({ id: id, ok: false, message: String(err && err.message || err) });
  }
};`;
try{
  worker = new Worker(URL.createObjectURL(new Blob([workerSrc], {type:'application/javascript'})));
}catch(e){ worker = null; }

let reqId = 0;
function workerValidate(text){
  return new Promise(resolve => {
    if(!worker){
      try{ JSON.parse(text); resolve({ok:true}); }
      catch(err){ resolve({ok:false, message:String(err.message||err)}); }
      return;
    }
    const id = ++reqId;
    const handler = ev => {
      if(ev.data.id !== id) return;
      worker.removeEventListener('message', handler);
      resolve(ev.data);
    };
    worker.addEventListener('message', handler);
    worker.postMessage({id, text});
    setTimeout(()=>{ worker.removeEventListener('message', handler); resolve({ok:false, message:'timeout'}); }, 8000);
  });
}

/* ================= ERROR POSITION ================= */
function locateError(message, text){
  let pos = -1, line = -1, col = -1;
  const pm = /position\s+(\d+)/i.exec(message);
  if(pm) pos = parseInt(pm[1], 10);
  const lm = /line\s+(\d+)\s+column\s+(\d+)/i.exec(message);
  if(lm){ line = parseInt(lm[1],10); col = parseInt(lm[2],10); }
  if(pos >= 0 && line < 0){
    const before = text.slice(0, pos);
    const lines = before.split('\n');
    line = lines.length;
    col = lines[lines.length-1].length + 1;
  }
  return { pos, line, col };
}

const FIX_HINTS = [
  { test: /trailing comma/i, hint: 'Remove the trailing comma before a closing bracket.' },
  { test: /Unexpected token '?'/i, hint: 'Check for a stray character ‚Äî often a comment or an unquoted key.' },
  { test: /Unexpected token }/i, hint: 'A trailing comma or an empty slot is likely sitting right before this brace.' },
  { test: /Unexpected token '\]'/i, hint: 'Trailing comma detected before a closing bracket.' },
  { test: /Expected property name/i, hint: 'Object keys must be wrapped in double quotes ‚Äî e.g. { "key": 1 }.' },
  { test: /Unexpected token '\\''/i, hint: 'JSON only allows double quotes. Convert single quotes to " ".', },
  { test: /Unexpected end of JSON input/i, hint: 'The input ended early ‚Äî a closing } or ] is missing.' },
  { test: /Unexpected non-whitespace/i, hint: 'Extra content after the JSON root. Only one root value is allowed.' }
];
function suggestFix(msg){
  for(const f of FIX_HINTS) if(f.test.test(msg)) return f.hint;
  return 'Run Auto-Fix to repair the most common JSON syntax issues in one pass.';
}

/* ================= AUTOFIX ================= */
function autoFix(src){
  let out = '', i = 0, n = src.length;
  src = src.replace(/^\uFEFF/, '');
  n = src.length;
  while(i < n){
    const c = src[i];
    if(c === '/' && src[i+1] === '/'){
      while(i < n && src[i] !== '\n') i++;
      continue;
    }
    if(c === '/' && src[i+1] === '*'){
      i += 2;
      while(i < n && !(src[i] === '*' && src[i+1] === '/')) i++;
      i += 2; continue;
    }
    if(c === '"' || c === "'"){
      const quote = c;
      let str = '';
      i++;
      while(i < n){
        const ch = src[i];
        if(ch === '\\'){ str += ch + (src[i+1] === undefined ? '' : src[i+1]); i += 2; continue; }
        if(ch === quote){ i++; break; }
        if(ch === '\n'){ str += '\\n'; i++; continue; }
        if(ch === '\r'){ i++; continue; }
        if(ch === '"'){ str += '\\"'; i++; continue; }
        str += ch; i++;
      }
      out += '"' + str + '"';
      continue;
    }
    if(/[A-Za-z_$]/.test(c)){
      let j = i;
      while(j < n && /[A-Za-z0-9_$]/.test(src[j])) j++;
      const word = src.slice(i, j);
      let k = j;
      while(k < n && /\s/.test(src[k])) k++;
      if(src[k] === ':'){
        out += '"' + word + '"';
        i = j; continue;
      }
      const low = word.toLowerCase();
      if(low === 'true' || low === 'false') out += low;
      else if(low === 'null' || low === 'none' || low === 'nil' || low === 'undefined') out += 'null';
      else out += '"' + word + '"';
      i = j; continue;
    }
    if(c === ','){
      let k = i + 1;
      while(k < n && /\s/.test(src[k])) k++;
      if(src[k] === '}' || src[k] === ']'){ i++; continue; }
    }
    out += c; i++;
  }
  return out;
}

/* ================= CONVERTERS ================= */
function toYAML(v, indent){
  indent = indent || 0;
  const pad = '  '.repeat(indent);
  const isObj = v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v);
  if(Array.isArray(v)){
    if(v.length === 0) return '[]';
    return v.map(item => {
      const child = toYAML(item, indent + 1);
      if(item && typeof item === 'object') return pad + '- ' + child.replace(/^\s+/, '');
      return pad + '- ' + child;
    }).join('\n');
  }
  if(isObj){
    const keys = Object.keys(v);
    if(keys.length === 0) return '{}';
    return keys.map(k => {
      const val = v[k];
      if(val && typeof val === 'object'){
        const nested = toYAML(val, indent + 1);
        return pad + k + ':\n' + (Array.isArray(val)
          ? nested.split('\n').map(l => '  ' + l).join('\n')
          : nested);
      }
      return pad + k + ': ' + yamlScalar(val);
    }).join('\n');
  }
  return pad + yamlScalar(v);
}
function yamlScalar(v){
  if(v === null) return 'null';
  if(typeof v === 'number' || typeof v === 'boolean') return String(v);
  const s = String(v);
  if(s === '' || /[:#\-\[\]{}&*!|>'"%@`,\n]/.test(s) || /^(true|false|null|~|\d)/i.test(s)){
    return JSON.stringify(s);
  }
  return s;
}
function csvCell(v){
  if(v === null || v === undefined) return '';
  let s = typeof v === 'object' ? JSON.stringify(v) : String(v);
  if(/[",\n\r]/.test(s)) s = '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"';
  return s;
}
function toCSV(data){
  let rows = [];
  if(Array.isArray(data)){
    if(data.length && data.every(r => r && typeof r === 'object' && !Array.isArray(r))){
      const headers = [];
      data.forEach(o => Object.keys(o).forEach(k => { if(headers.indexOf(k) === -1) headers.push(k); }));
      rows.push(headers.map(csvCell).join(','));
      data.forEach(o => rows.push(headers.map(h => csvCell(o[h])).join(',')));
    } else if(data.every(r => Array.isArray(r))){
      data.forEach(r => rows.push(r.map(csvCell).join(',')));
    } else {
      data.forEach(r => rows.push(csvCell(r)));
    }
  } else if(data && typeof data === 'object'){
    const keys = Object.keys(data);
    rows.push('key,value');
    keys.forEach(k => rows.push(csvCell(k) + ',' + csvCell(data[k])));
  } else {
    rows.push(csvCell(data));
  }
  return rows.join('\n');
}
function xmlEscape(s){
  return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, c => ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&apos;'}[c]));
}
function sanitizeTag(k){
  let t = String(k).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_\-.]/g, '_');
  if(!/^[A-Za-z_]/.test(t)) t = '_' + t;
  return t;
}
function toXML(v, tag, depth){
  tag = tag || 'root'; depth = depth || 0;
  const pad = '  '.repeat(depth);
  if(Array.isArray(v)){
    const inner = v.map(item => toXML(item, 'item', depth + 1)).join('\n');
    return pad + '<' + tag + '>\n' + inner + '\n' + pad + '</' + tag + '>';
  }
  if(v && typeof v === 'object'){
    const inner = Object.keys(v).map(k => toXML(v[k], sanitizeTag(k), depth + 1)).join('\n');
    return pad + '<' + tag + '>\n' + inner + '\n' + pad + '</' + tag + '>';
  }
  if(v === null) return pad + '<' + tag + '/>';
  return pad + '<' + tag + '>' + xmlEscape(v) + '</' + tag + '>';
}
function toXMLDoc(data){ return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n' + toXML(data); }

/* ================= TREE ================= */
function treeHTML(v, key, depth){
  const isArr = Array.isArray(v);
  const isObj = v && typeof v === 'object' && !isArr;
  if(isArr || isObj){
    const entries = isArr ? v.map((x, idx) => [idx, x]) : Object.keys(v).map(k => [k, v[k]]);
    const open = depth < 2 ? ' open' : '';
    const br = isArr ? ['[', ']'] : ['{', '}'];
    let h = '<div class="tnode' + open + '">';
    h += '<div class="trow"><span class="tcaret"></span>';
    if(key !== null && key !== undefined){
      h += '<span class="tkey">' + esc(String(key)) + '</span><span class="tcolon">:</span>';
    }
    h += '<span class="tbrace">' + br[0] + '</span>';
    h += '<span class="tmeta">' + entries.length + ' ' + (isArr ? 'item' : 'key') + (entries.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + '</span>';
    h += '</div><div class="tchildren">';
    for(let e = 0; e < entries.length; e++){
      h += treeHTML(entries[e][1], entries[e][0], depth + 1);
    }
    h += '</div><div class="trow tclose"><span class="tcaret ghost"></span><span class="tbrace">' + br[1] + '</span></div></div>';
    return h;
  }
  let cls = 'tok-null', disp = 'null';
  if(v === null || v === undefined){ cls = 'tok-null'; disp = 'null'; }
  else if(typeof v === 'string'){ cls = 'tok-str'; disp = '"' + v + '"'; }
  else if(typeof v === 'number'){ cls = 'tok-num'; disp = String(v); }
  else if(typeof v === 'boolean'){ cls = 'tok-bool'; disp = String(v); }
  else { disp = String(v); }
  let h = '<div class="tleaf">';
  if(key !== null && key !== undefined){
    h += '<span class="tkey">' + esc(String(key)) + '</span><span class="tcolon">:</span>';
  }
  h += '<span class="' + cls + '">' + esc(disp) + '</span></div>';
  return h;
}
$('#treeView').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.trow');
  if(!row || row.classList.contains('tclose')) return;
  const node = row.parentElement;
  if(node && node.classList.contains('tnode')) node.classList.toggle('open');
});

/* ================= RENDER OUTPUT ================= */
function renderOutput(){
  if(state.mode === 'tree'){
    els.out.style.display = 'none';
    els.outGutterWrap.style.display = 'none';
    els.tree.style.display = 'block';
    if(state.valid){
      els.tree.innerHTML = treeHTML(state.parsed, null, 0);
    } else {
      els.tree.innerHTML = '<div style="color:var(--dim);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:13px;padding:8px 0">No valid JSON to visualize yet. Fix the input to explore the tree.</div>';
    }
    return;
  }
  els.tree.style.display = 'none';
  els.out.style.display = 'block';
  els.outGutterWrap.style.display = 'block';
  const text = state.rawOutput || '';
  els.out.innerHTML = text ? highlightJson(text) + '\n' : '';
  els.outGutter.innerHTML = text ? gutterHTML(text, -1) : '<div>1</div>';
  els.out.scrollTop = 0;
  els.outGutterWrap.scrollTop = 0;
}
$('#outPre').addEventListener('scroll', e => {
  els.outGutterWrap.scrollTop = e.target.scrollTop;
}, {passive:true});

/* ================= MAIN PIPELINE ================= */
let debounceTimer = null;
function schedule(){
  clearTimeout(debounceTimer);
  debounceTimer = setTimeout(run, 220);
}

async function run(){
  const text = els.ta.value;
  const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(text).length;
  const lines = text ? text.split('\n').length : 0;

  if(!text.trim()){
    state.valid = false; state.parsed = undefined; state.rawOutput = ''; state.errorLine = -1;
    els.badge.className = 'valid-badge idle'; els.badge.textContent = 'Ready';
    els.errBar.classList.remove('show');
    els.stats.innerHTML = '<span><b>0</b> chars</span><span><b>0</b> bytes</span><span><b>0</b> lines</span>';
    renderOutput();
    return;
  }

  const res = await workerValidate(text);
  els.stats.innerHTML = '<span><b>' + text.length.toLocaleString() + '</b> chars</span>' +
                        '<span><b>' + bytes.toLocaleString() + '</b> bytes</span>' +
                        '<span><b>' + lines.toLocaleString() + '</b> lines</span>';

  if(res.ok){
    try{
      state.parsed = JSON.parse(text);
      state.valid = true;
      state.lastError = null;
      state.errorLine = -1;
      const pretty = JSON.stringify(state.parsed, null, state.indent);
      if(state.mode !== 'tree') state.rawOutput = pretty;
      els.badge.className = 'valid-badge ok';
      els.badge.textContent = '‚úì Valid JSON';
      els.errBar.classList.remove('show');
      els.outLabel.textContent = state.mode === 'tree' ? 'Tree Visualizer' : 'Formatted Output';
    }catch(e){
      state.valid = false;
    }
  } else {
    state.valid = false;
    state.parsed = undefined;
    const info = locateError(res.message || '', text);
    state.errorLine = info.line > 0 ? info.line : -1;
    state.lastError = { message: res.message, line: info.line, col: info.col };
    els.badge.className = 'valid-badge bad';
    els.badge.textContent = '‚úï Invalid JSON';
    const loc = info.line > 0 ? ('Line ' + info.line + (info.col > 0 ? ', Column ' + info.col : '') + ' ‚Äî ') : '';
    els.errMsg.innerHTML = '<b>' + loc + '</b><code>' + esc(String(res.message || '').slice(0, 180)) + '</code><br>' + esc(suggestFix(String(res.message || '')));
    els.errBar.classList.add('show');
    els.outLabel.textContent = 'Formatted Output';
  }
  renderInput();
  renderOutput();
}

/* ================= ACTIONS ================= */
function setInput(text){
  els.ta.value = text;
  renderInput();
  run();
}

const actions = {
  prettify(){
    if(!state.valid){ toast('‚ö† Fix the JSON first ‚Äî or hit Auto-Fix'); return; }
    state.mode = 'code'; setSegment('code');
    state.rawOutput = JSON.stringify(state.parsed, null, state.indent);
    renderOutput();
    toast('‚ú® Prettified with ' + state.indent + ' spaces');
  },
  minify(){
    if(!state.valid){ toast('‚ö† Fix the JSON first ‚Äî or hit Auto-Fix'); return; }
    state.mode = 'code'; setSegment('code');
    const min = JSON.stringify(state.parsed);
    state.rawOutput = min;
    renderOutput();
    toast('‚ö° Minified ‚Äî saved ' + Math.max(0, (els.ta.value.length - min.length)).toLocaleString() + ' chars');
  },
  async validate(){
    await run();
    toast(state.valid ? '‚úì Valid JSON ‚Äî no errors found' : '‚úï Invalid JSON ‚Äî see the error bar');
  },
  autofix(){
    const src = els.ta.value;
    if(!src.trim()){ toast('Nothing to fix'); return; }
    const fixed = autoFix(src);
    let ok = false, parsed;
    try{ parsed = JSON.parse(fixed); ok = true; }catch(e){ ok = false; }
    if(!ok){
      const fixed2 = autoFix(fixed);
      try{ parsed = JSON.parse(fixed2); ok = true; setInput(JSON.stringify(parsed, null, state.indent)); toast('‚ö° Auto-Fix applied'); return; }
      catch(e){ setInput(fixed); toast('‚ö† Partial fix applied ‚Äî check the error bar'); return; }
    }
    setInput(JSON.stringify(parsed, null, state.indent));
    toast('‚ö° Auto-Fix applied successfully');
  },
  async copy(){
    const text = state.mode === 'tree' ? JSON.stringify(state.parsed, null, state.indent) : (state.rawOutput || els.ta.value);
    if(!text){ toast('Nothing to copy'); return; }
    try{
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(text);
      toast('üìã Copied to clipboard');
    }catch(e){
      const t = document.createElement('textarea');
      t.value = text; document.body.appendChild(t); t.select();
      document.execCommand('copy'); document.body.removeChild(t);
      toast('üìã Copied to clipboard');
    }
  },
  download(){
    if(!state.valid){ toast('‚ö† Nothing valid to download'); return; }
    const choice = prompt('Download format ‚Äî type: json, yaml, csv, or xml', 'json');
    if(!choice) return;
    const f = choice.trim().toLowerCase();
    let content = '', mime = 'text/plain', ext = f;
    if(f === 'json'){ content = JSON.stringify(state.parsed, null, state.indent); mime = 'application/json'; ext = 'json'; }
    else if(f === 'yaml' || f === 'yml'){ content = toYAML(state.parsed, 0); mime = 'text/yaml'; ext = 'yaml'; }
    else if(f === 'csv'){ content = toCSV(state.parsed); mime = 'text/csv'; ext = 'csv'; }
    else if(f === 'xml'){ content = toXMLDoc(state.parsed); mime = 'application/xml'; ext = 'xml'; }
    else { toast('Unknown format'); return; }
    const blob = new Blob([content], {type: mime + ';charset=utf-8'});
    const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement('a');
    a.href = url; a.download = 'data.' + ext;
    document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); document.body.removeChild(a);
    setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 1500);
    toast('‚¨á Downloaded data.' + ext);
  },
  async share(){
    if(!state.valid){ toast('‚ö† Fix the JSON first'); return; }
    const json = JSON.stringify(state.parsed);
    if(json.length > 120000){ toast('‚ö† Too large for a share link ‚Äî download instead'); return; }
    try{
      const bytes = new TextEncoder().encode(json);
      let payload;
      if('CompressionStream' in window){
        const cs = new CompressionStream('gzip');
        const stream = new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(cs);
        const buf = new Uint8Array(await new Response(stream).arrayBuffer());
        payload = 'g.' + b64url(buf);
      } else {
        payload = 'b.' + b64url(bytes);
      }
      const url = location.origin + location.pathname + '#d=' + payload;
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(url);
      toast('üîó Share link copied (' + url.length + ' chars)');
    }catch(e){ toast('‚ö† Could not generate link'); }
  },
  sample(){
    setInput(JSON.stringify({
      team: "JSONForge",
      version: "2.4.0",
      private: true,
      stars: 28450,
      maintainers: [
        { name: "Ada", role: "core", commits: 1842 },
        { name: "Linus", role: "infra", commits: 967 }
      ],
      features: ["format", "validate", "tree-view", "yaml", "csv", "xml"],
      config: { indent: 2, theme: "dark", worker: true, limit: "10MB" },
      lastRelease: null
    }, null, 2));
    toast('Sample payload loaded');
  },
  clear(){
    setInput('');
    state.parsed = undefined; state.valid = false; state.rawOutput = ''; state.errorLine = -1;
    els.badge.className = 'valid-badge idle'; els.badge.textContent = 'Ready';
    els.errBar.classList.remove('show');
    els.stats.innerHTML = '';
    renderOutput();
    toast('Cleared');
  }
};

function b64url(u8){
  let s = '';
  const chunk = 0x8000;
  for(let i = 0; i < u8.length; i += chunk){
    s += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, u8.subarray(i, i + chunk));
  }
  return btoa(s).replace(/\+/g,'-').replace(/\//g,'_').replace(/=+$/,'');
}
function b64urlDecode(str){
  let s = str.replace(/-/g,'+').replace(/_/g,'/');
  while(s.length % 4) s += '=';
  const bin = atob(s);
  const u8 = new Uint8Array(bin.length);
  for(let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) u8[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i);
  return u8;
}

document.querySelector('.toolbar').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const btn = e.target.closest('[data-act]');
  if(!btn) return;
  const act = btn.getAttribute('data-act');
  if(actions[act]) actions[act]();
});
document.querySelector('.fixnow').addEventListener('click', () => actions.autofix());

function setSegment(mode){
  $$('#viewSeg button').forEach(b => b.classList.toggle('on', b.getAttribute('data-mode') === mode));
}
$('#viewSeg').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const btn = e.target.closest('[data-mode]');
  if(!btn) return;
  state.mode = btn.getAttribute('data-mode');
  setSegment(state.mode);
  if(state.mode === 'tree' && state.valid){
    state.rawOutput = JSON.stringify(state.parsed, null, state.indent);
  }
  renderOutput();
  els.outLabel.textContent = state.mode === 'tree' ? 'Tree Visualizer' : 'Formatted Output';
});

/* ================= KEYBOARD SHORTCUTS ================= */
document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
  const mod = e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey;
  if(!mod) return;
  if(e.key === 'Enter'){
    e.preventDefault();
    if(!state.valid && els.ta.value.trim()) actions.autofix();
    else actions.prettify();
  } else if(e.shiftKey && (e.key === 'C' || e.key === 'c')){
    e.preventDefault();
    actions.copy();
  } else if(e.shiftKey && (e.key === 'F' || e.key === 'f')){
    e.preventDefault();
    actions.autofix();
  } else if(e.shiftKey && (e.key === 'M' || e.key === 'm')){
    e.preventDefault();
    actions.minify();
  }
});

/* ================= DRAG & DROP ================= */
const dropZone = document.querySelector('.tool-shell');
['dragenter','dragover'].forEach(ev => dropZone.addEventListener(ev, e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  dropZone.style.borderColor = 'var(--accent)';
}));
['dragleave','drop'].forEach(ev => dropZone.addEventListener(ev, e => {
  e.preventDefault();
  dropZone.style.borderColor = 'var(--border)';
}));
dropZone.addEventListener('drop', e => {
  const file = e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files[0];
  if(!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = () => { setInput(String(reader.result)); toast('üìÑ ' + file.name + ' loaded'); };
  reader.readAsText(file);
});

/* ================= FAQ ACCORDION ================= */
$$('.fq').forEach(btn => {
  btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
    const item = btn.parentElement;
    const body = item.querySelector('.fa');
    const isOpen = item.classList.contains('open');
    $$('.fitem').forEach(f => {
      f.classList.remove('open');
      f.querySelector('.fa').style.maxHeight = null;
    });
    if(!isOpen){
      item.classList.add('open');
      body.style.maxHeight = body.scrollHeight + 'px';
    }
  });
});

/* ================= SHARE LINK HYDRATION ================= */
(async function hydrate(){
  const hash = location.hash;
  if(hash.indexOf('#d=') !== 0) return;
  try{
    const raw = hash.slice(3);
    const kind = raw.slice(0, 1);
    const body = raw.slice(2);
    const bytes = b64urlDecode(body);
    let json;
    if(kind === 'g' && 'DecompressionStream' in window){
      const ds = new DecompressionStream('gzip');
      const stream = new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(ds);
      json = await new Response(stream).text();
    } else {
      json = new TextDecoder().decode(bytes);
    }
    setInput(json);
    toast('üîó Shared payload loaded');
  }catch(e){ /* silent */ }
})();

/* ================= BOOT ================= */
renderInput();
renderOutput();
run();

})();
</script>
</body>
</html>
